775: export const evaluateThaiCarryoverForEnglish = (
776:   groups: Array<{ sourceText: string }>,
777:   translatedGroups: string[],
778:   targetLanguage: string
779: ): { shouldFallback: boolean; thaiRatio: number; carryoverRatio: number; total: number } => {
780:   const normalizedTarget = normalizeTranslationLanguage(targetLanguage, 'en');
781:   if (normalizedTarget !== 'en') {
782:     return { shouldFallback: false, thaiRatio: 0, carryoverRatio: 0, total: 0 };
783:   }
784: 
785:   let total = 0;
786:   let carryoverCount = 0;
787:   let thaiChars = 0;
788:   let letterChars = 0;
789: 
790:   groups.forEach((group, i) => {
791:     const translated = translatedGroups[i] || '';
792:     total += 1;
793:     
794:     // If target is English, translated text should contain minimal Thai
795:     const stats = getTranslationScriptStats(translated);
796:     const hasThai = stats.thaiChars > 0;
797:     if (hasThai && stats.thaiRatio >= 0.3) {
798:       carryoverCount += 1;
799:     }
800:     thaiChars += stats.thaiChars;
801:     letterChars += stats.letterChars;
802:   });
803: 
804:   if (total === 0) {
805:     return { shouldFallback: false, thaiRatio: 0, carryoverRatio: 0, total };
806:   }
807: 
808:   const carryoverRatio = carryoverCount / total;
809:   const thaiRatio = thaiChars / Math.max(1, letterChars);
810:   const shouldFallback = carryoverRatio >= 0.5 && thaiRatio >= 0.25;
811:   return { shouldFallback, thaiRatio, carryoverRatio, total };
812: };

775: export const evaluateThaiCarryoverForEnglish = (
776:   groups: Array<{ sourceText: string }>,
777:   translatedGroups: string[],
778:   targetLanguage: string
779: ): { shouldFallback: boolean; thaiRatio: number; carryoverRatio: number; total: number } => {
780:   const normalizedTarget = normalizeTranslationLanguage(targetLanguage, 'en');
781:   if (normalizedTarget !== 'en') {
782:     return { shouldFallback: false, thaiRatio: 0, carryoverRatio: 0, total: 0 };
783:   }
784: 
785:   let total = 0;
786:   let carryoverCount = 0;
787:   let thaiChars = 0;
788:   let letterChars = 0;
789: 
790:   groups.forEach((group, i) => {
791:     const translated = translatedGroups[i] || '';
792:     total += 1;
793:     
794:     // If target is English, translated text should contain minimal Thai
795:     const stats = getTranslationScriptStats(translated);
796:     const hasThai = stats.thaiChars > 0;
797:     if (hasThai && stats.thaiRatio >= 0.3) {
798:       carryoverCount += 1;
799:     }
800:     thaiChars += stats.thaiChars;
801:     letterChars += stats.letterChars;
802:   });
803: 
804:   if (total === 0) {
805:     return { shouldFallback: false, thaiRatio: 0, carryoverRatio: 0, total };
806:   }
807: 
808:   const carryoverRatio = carryoverCount / total;
809:   const thaiRatio = thaiChars / Math.max(1, letterChars);
810:   const shouldFallback = carryoverRatio >= 0.5 && thaiRatio >= 0.25;
811:   return { shouldFallback, thaiRatio, carryoverRatio, total };
812: };

9: // ─── Prepared Statement Cache ──────────────────────────────────────────────
10: // Avoid recompiling hot-path SQL on every call (OCR cache read/write, translation cache).
11: // Statements are lazily compiled once and reused across calls.

14: function getStmt(sql: string): Database.Statement {
15:   if (!db) throw new Error('DB not initialized');

17:   if (!stmt) {
18:     stmt = db.prepare(sql);
19:     stmtCache.set(sql, stmt);
20:   }
21:   return stmt;
22: }

278:   texts: string[],
279:   type: string,
280:   mode: TranslationMode,
281:   sourceLanguage: string,

283: ): Promise<string[]> {

296:

775: export const evaluateThaiCarryoverForEnglish = (
776:   groups: Array<{ sourceText: string }>,
777:   translatedGroups: string[],
778:   targetLanguage: string
779: ): { shouldFallback: boolean; thaiRatio: number; carryoverRatio: number; total: number } => {
780:   const normalizedTarget = normalizeTranslationLanguage(targetLanguage, 'en');
781:   if (normalizedTarget !== 'en') {
782:     return { shouldFallback: false, thaiRatio: 0, carryoverRatio: 0, total: 0 };
783:   }
784: 
785:   let total = 0;
786:   let carryoverCount = 0;
787:   let thaiChars = 0;
788:   let letterChars = 0;
789: 
790:   groups.forEach((group, i) => {
791:     const translated = translatedGroups[i] || '';
792:     total += 1;
793:     
794:     // If target is English, translated text should contain minimal Thai
795:     const stats = getTranslationScriptStats(translated);
796:     const hasThai = stats.thaiChars > 0;
797:     if (hasThai && stats.thaiRatio >= 0.3) {
798:       carryoverCount += 1;
799:     }
800:     thaiChars += stats.thaiChars;
801:     letterChars += stats.letterChars;
802:   });
803: 
804:   if (total === 0) {
805:     return { shouldFallback: false, thaiRatio: 0, carryoverRatio: 0, total };
806:   }
807: 
808:   const carryoverRatio = carryoverCount / total;
809:   const thaiRatio = thaiChars / Math.max(1, letterChars);
810:   const shouldFallback = carryoverRatio >= 0.5 && thaiRatio >= 0.25;
811:   return { shouldFallback, thaiRatio, carryoverRatio, total };
812: };

279:   type: string,
280:   mode: TranslationMode,
281:   sourceLanguage: string,
282:   targetLanguage: string
283: ): Promise<string[]> {
284:   if (texts.length === 0) return [];
285: 
286:   if (!window.electronAPI?.translate?.offlineBatch) {
287:     throw new Error('Offline translation bridge is not available.');
288:   }
289: 
290:   const translated = await window.electronAPI.translate.offlineBatch(texts, {
291:     mode,
292:     sourceType: type,
293:     sourceLanguage,
294:     targetLanguage,
295:   });
296: 
297:   if (!Array.isArray(translated) || translated.length !== texts.length) {
298:     throw new Error('Offline translation returned an invalid result length.');
299:   }
300: 
301:   return translated;
302: }

738: export const evaluateUntranslatedTranslation = (
739:   lines: Array<{ index: number; text: string }>,
740:   translatedByIndex: Map<number, string>,
741:   sourceLanguage: string,
742:   targetLanguage: string
743: ): { shouldSkip: boolean; retryRatio: number; thaiRatio: number; total: number } => {
744:   const normalizedTarget = normalizeTranslationLanguage(targetLanguage, 'th');
745:   if (normalizedTarget !== 'th') {
746:     return { shouldSkip: false, retryRatio: 0, thaiRatio: 0, total: 0 };
747:   }
748: 
749:   let total = 0;
750:   let retryCount = 0;
751:   let thaiChars = 0;
752:   let letterChars = 0;
753: 
754:   lines.forEach((line) => {
755:     const translated = translatedByIndex.get(line.index) || '';
756:     total += 1;
757:     if (shouldRetryLineTranslation(line.text, translated, sourceLanguage, normalizedTarget)) {
758:       retryCount += 1;
759:     }
760:     const stats = getTranslationScriptStats(translated);
761:     thaiChars += stats.thaiChars;
762:     letterChars += stats.letterChars;
763:   });
764: 
765:   if (total < 4) {
766:     return { shouldSkip: false, retryRatio: 0, thaiRatio: 0, total };
767:   }
768: 
769:   const retryRatio = retryCount / Math.max(1, total);
770:   const thaiRatio = thaiChars / Math.max(1, letterChars);
771:   const shouldSkip = retryRatio >= 0.65 && thaiRatio < 0.06;
772:   return { shouldSkip, retryRatio, thaiRatio, total };
773: };
